- **research-reviewer**: Tries to refute a JustUI research report claim by claim - opens each source, checks it says what the claim says, checks the version matches the repo, and looks for counter-evidence. - **research-reviewer**: Independently review a supplied research artifact and its evidence for concrete scientific defects. Use when an independent review is requested. ([bsq415/research-rigor-skill](https://github.com/bsq415/research-rigor-skill/blob/HEAD/agents/research-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **research-reviewer**: Use this agent when a research phase has been completed and needs to be reviewed for scientific rigor, statistical validity, and publication readiness. Examples: &lt;example&gt;Context: Baseline experiments completed. user: "I've finished running all baseline comparisons as outlined in the experiment design" assistant: "Let me dispatch the research-reviewer to evaluate the results against the design document" &lt;commentary&gt;Since a major research phase has been completed, use the research-reviewer agent to validate the work against the registered design and scientific standards.&lt;/commentary&gt;&lt;/example&gt;… ([jeonnoin-alt/Eureka](https://github.com/jeonnoin-alt/Eureka/blob/HEAD/agents/research-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **research-reviewer**: MUST BE USED PROACTIVELY after writing or modifying any research code. Reviews against reproducibility standards, numerical correctness, data leakage, and Python best practices. Checks for common research pitfalls and anti-patterns. ([enlihhhhh/claude-code-research-skills](https://github.com/enlihhhhh/claude-code-research-skills/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/research-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **research-reviewer**: RESEARCH team reviewer — paper analysis, literature synthesis, data analysis, experiment tracking ([jaminitachi/SuperClaw](https://github.com/jaminitachi/SuperClaw/blob/HEAD/agents/research-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **research-reviewer**: Evaluates design decisions, open-ended questions, and trade-offs against engineering and design perspectives. Unlike code-reviewer (diffs) and plan-reviewer (structured plans), research-reviewer handles research questions and recommendations. ([simonedjb/seja](https://github.com/simonedjb/seja/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/research-reviewer.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **research-reviewer**: Roda no Stage 1 após o passo de revisão de datas (`scripts/research-review-dates.ts`, ([vjpixel/diaria-studio](https://github.com/vjpixel/diaria-studio/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/research-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **research-reviewer**: 质量评估的唯一执行者，双模式运行：充分性评估（Sufficiency Gate，G1/G2/G3） 和 6 维度审阅（Validation，Stage 6）。当 Lead 需要判定当前 Stage 产物是否 达到进入下一阶段的门槛，或对综合产物/deliverables 草稿做准确性、完整性、 逻辑性、可操作性、可追溯性的审阅并给出 verdict 时，spawn 此 subagent。 典型触发场景：Acquisition+Sanitization 完成后的 G1 评估、Decomposition 完成后的 G2 评估、Synthesis 完成后的 G3 评估（含假设审计与证伪审计）、 Stage 6 对最终产物的对抗审阅。不负责 G0 需求门（由 Lead 与用户对齐）。 ([WooDragon/cc-plugins](https://github.com/WooDragon/cc-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/deep-research/agents/research-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **research-reviewer**: Reviews research questions, argument direction, scope, evidence sufficiency, and missing counterarguments for generic writing projects. ([2ndelement/writing-research-template](https://github.com/2ndelement/writing-research-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/research-reviewer.md))
- **research-reviewer**: Critique methods, reasoning, evaluation design, and conclusions in research artifacts. Use for an independent assessment of an analysis, research brief, manuscript, or simulation study, with findings tied to evidence and practical remedies. ([cameronbracken/waterology-research](https://github.com/cameronbracken/waterology-research/blob/HEAD/.agent/agents/research-reviewer.md) · 3 copies)
- **research-reviewer**: Use this agent when you need adversarial peer review of machine learning or data science research documents — paper drafts, technical reports, research summaries, or experiment writeups. Unlike a standard editorial pass, this agent audits every claim for prior art, practitioner obviousness, factual correctness, and tautology before evaluating execution, then delivers a structured review with an explicit disposition (submit / reframe / kill). Invoke it before submitting to a venue, before investing further in a research direction, or whenever you need to know whether the contributions are real… ([chris-santiago/ml-lab](https://github.com/chris-santiago/ml-lab/blob/HEAD/plugins/ml-lab/agents/research-reviewer.md))
- **research-reviewer**: 独立审查 investigation/ 下研报、跟踪手册与投研汇总，核验其是否符合 deep_investment_analysis_framework.md 框架要求，识别乐观偏差、遗漏利空、逻辑缺陷与事实错误，并审计更新形态与文档整洁度（累积式更新/更新日志体/追加式堆叠）。当用户要求审查、复核或评估研报及跟踪手册质量时使用。 ([hrzzzrh/QuantPyLab](https://github.com/hrzzzrh/QuantPyLab/blob/HEAD/.zcode-plugin/agents/research-reviewer.md) · 4 copies)
- **research-reviewer**: Use after technical-researcher (external mode) or codebase-analyst (codebase mode) produces a research note — reads the note with fresh, independent context and produces a structured objection record. Two modes: external challenges source quality, claim-source mismatch, assumptions, confidence, gaps; codebase challenges evidence mismatch, missed counterexamples, pattern overfitting, scope creep, impression-without-citation. Read-only trust boundary enforces the human-cognition gate on dispositions. This is the research pipeline's equivalent of advocatus-diaboli. Examples: &lt;example&gt; Context: t… ([jrgrant/fedora-cosmic-atomic-dx](https://github.com/jrgrant/fedora-cosmic-atomic-dx/blob/HEAD/.copilot/plugins/ai-literacy-superpowers/ai-literacy-superpowers/agents/research-reviewer.agent.md))
- **research-reviewer**: Hostile but fair reviewer for ML experiment results in a research-lab repo. Use before any result enters RESEARCH_LOG.md or FINDINGS.md. Give it the experiment path (experiments/NNN-slug). Returns ACCEPT / REVISE / REJECT with numbered, file-cited objections. Never fixes anything. ([siinghd/skills](https://github.com/siinghd/skills/blob/HEAD/agents/research-reviewer.md))
- **research-reviewer**: Adversarial fact-checker for research briefs. Verifies each incoming claim against its cited source and lists gaps. Use as the start-research reviewer after the lens agents have written their briefs. ([szymonrychu/workbench](https://github.com/szymonrychu/workbench/blob/HEAD/plugins/workbench/agents/research-reviewer.md))
- **research-reviewer-agent**: You are a forensic reviewer for causal signal research experiments. You audit for temporal correctness, accounting correctness, and verification completeness. You are READ-ONLY. ([adam-s/alphadidactic](https://github.com/adam-s/alphadidactic/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/research-reviewer-agent.md) · ★75)
- **research-reviewer-lite**: Use this agent when you need rigorous academic peer review of machine learning or data science research documents, including paper drafts, technical reports, research summaries, or experiment writeups. Invoke it when you want structured, actionable feedback organized into summary, strengths, critical issues, and prioritized recommendations — especially before submitting to a venue, sharing with collaborators, or finalizing a technical report. &lt;example&gt; Context: The user has just finished drafting a machine learning paper and wants feedback before submission to NeurIPS. user: "I've finished my… ([chris-santiago/ml-lab](https://github.com/chris-santiago/ml-lab/blob/HEAD/plugins/ml-lab/agents/research-reviewer-lite.md))
- **research-reviewer-narrative**: Use AFTER research synthesis to produce the narrative portion of Research-Review (User Impact Analysis, Scope Recommendation, Prioritization Framework, Stakeholder Summary). The graded PM1-PM11 scorecard is handled by separate dim subagents, not by this one. Dispatched by `/research` Phase 2 review. ([infraspecdev/tesseract](https://github.com/infraspecdev/tesseract/blob/HEAD/shield/agents/research-reviewer-narrative.md) · ★6)
- **research-risks**: Use when identifying edge cases, failure modes, and gotchas before implementing a feature — surfaces risks the implementation plan must address. ([itsacoyote/ai-config](https://github.com/itsacoyote/ai-config/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/research-risks.md))
- **research-rl-decision**: Orchestrator for the rl-decision loop. Runs the loop by delegating — populates the state via research agents, assembles the prompt, delegates the work to the `executor` agent — then asks the human a scored question itself (a subagent's question does not reach the user) and improves the prompt by a temporal-difference update until it converges. Use for decisions. It does NOT analyze or decide on its own. ([atomic-235/shared](https://github.com/atomic-235/shared/blob/HEAD/opencode/agents/research-rl-decision.md))
- **research-router**: Composes a priced research plan for the research-plays front door, which dispatches it on every research ask that reaches the pitch and on a Jev `none_fit` ask. It returns either the open questions it still needs answered or two to three priced options for the user to pick from (only options when handed Jev's survivors). It never asks the user anything and never calls the SocialCrawl API; it reads local files and returns its answer to the session that dispatched it, and that session does the asking. ([joeoliveimpact/revxl-marketplace](https://github.com/joeoliveimpact/revxl-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/socialcrawl-superengine/agents/research-router.md) · ★1)
- **research-routing**: This note supplements the repository's current [AGENTS.md](../../../AGENTS.md). AGENTS.md governs workforce profiles, authority, and permissions; follow it if guidance here conflicts. A role name or role file describes requested setup, not proof of what a child session actually received. ([tumlinso/project-control](https://github.com/tumlinso/project-control/blob/HEAD/archive/assistance-v0/docs/workflow_foundation_v2/agents/research-routing.md))

- **research-rules**: Apply to any change that trains, tunes, evaluates or reports a strategy. ([btamadio/rbit](https://github.com/btamadio/rbit/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/research-rules.md))
